Dear Jeffrey. 
Thank you for being a wonderful supporter of UniversalGiving"' (www.UniversalGiving.org). Due to your 
involvement, we are making an impact in over 100 countries, allowing thousands of people to give and 
volunteer internationally! We are pleased to tell you that we have been featured in CBS, FOX Business 
Network. Business Week, WomenEntrepreneur. Oprah.com, and The New York Times. 
The following is to give you an update on UniversalGiving's most significant events and partnerships of 
O1/O2 2011. These include our recent partnership with Symantec, our Commonwealth Club event, and our 
collaboration with Yonas Media for their worldwide music tour. Here's the summary of our exciting update: 
1. New Links For Helping Drought and Famine Victims in East Africa 
2. Fast Company Selects CEO Pamela Hawley as an Expert Blogger 
3. UniversalGiving Convenes Commonwealth Club Event on Global CSR 
4. UniversalGiving's Partnership Update: Symantec and Yonas Media 
5. CEO's Inspirational Story Highlighted in Living the Life of My Dreams 
I. Help Drought and Famine Victims in East Africa 
The United Nations recently declared a state of famine in Somalia. You can help the victims! Based on 
our extensive network with NGOs in East Africa. UniversalGiving has compiled opportunities and a 
spotlight article on our website to contribute. We have also been working with the Christian Science Monitor 
to spread awareness and encourage giving. 
II. Fast Company Selects CEO as an Expert Slogger 
Pamela Hawley, the CEO of UniversalGiving, was chosen as a Fast Company Expert Blogger on Corporate 
Social Responsibility. The following are four recent articles: 
1. Global CSR Part One' How You Scale Your Giving Internationally

3. Corporate Social Responsibility: How You Can Profit—And Kick Poverty Out 
4. Why I Think CEOs Should Ap'ooze 
III. UniversalGiving Convenes Commonwealth Club Event on Global 
Corporate Social Responsibility 
CEO Pamela Hawley interviews top executives from Deloitte, Hewlett-Packard, and Dell 
Pamela Hawley presented at the Commonwealth Club of California on "Global Corporate Social 
Responsibility: How You Can Scale Your Philanthropy and Volunteer Programs Worldwide- As told by CEOs 
& CSR Executives at Fortune 500 Companies." She interviewed top executives Mark Edmunds (Deloitte), 
Gabriele Zedlmayer (Hewlett-Packard) and Trisa Thompson (Dell) to discuss their latest innovations in Global 
CSR. 
IV. UniversalGiving's Partnership Update: Symantec and Yonas Media 
1. Symantec has chosen to partner with UniversalGiving Corporate. UniversalGiving Corporate is creating 
the strategy and execution for Symantec's Global Volunteer Program in more than 40 countries. 
UniversalGiving Corporate provides the strategic operations and communication plans to ensure Fortune 500 
Companies are global leaders in Corporate Social Responsibility. 
2. Yonas Media has launched a worldwide music tour and is partnering with UniversalGiving. 
The tour visits over 20 cities, and promotes a local non-profit in each. They encourage their audiences to 
support these non-profits. All donations are facilitated through our specially created Zion I and the Grouch 
splashpage on the UniversalGiving website. 
V. CEO's Inspirational Story Highlighted in Living the Life of My Dreams 
Our CEO's personal essay was recently featured in the book bong the Life of My Dreams. Pamela wrote 
about "A World of Harmonious Living.' where she discusses the inspirational moment she realized the 
importance of getting involved globally. If you choose to purchase the book through this link, UniversalGiving 
will receive 50% of the commission. 
VI. UniversalGiving Advises the Public about Japanese Crisis Relief on the 
Radio 
In a recent radio interview, CEO Pamela Hawley shared about UniversalGiving's involvement in Japanese 
crisis relief. She highlighted our key partners in this effort, such as Give2Asia International Medical Corps 
and Relief International.

Background Information on UniversalGiving 
UniversalGiving TM is an award-winning website allowing people to donate and volunteer with the top 
performing projects all over the world. Our projects are vetted through a proprietary Quality Model" to 
ensure the most effective, trustworthy philanthropy possible. Unique to UniversalGiving, we take no cut on 
donations. 100% of your donation goes directly to the cause. 
UniversalGiving has been profiled on CBS and in FOX Business Network and has also been featured in 
Business Week, WomenEntrepreneur, Oprah.corn, NBC News, The Christian Science Monitor, The New 
York Times, The Financial Times, Market Watch, CNNMoney.com, Change.org and See Change 
Magazine. 
CEO Pamela Hawley is a winner of the Jefferson Award (the Nobel Prize in Community Service) and a 
Colbum S. Wilbur Leadership Fellow at the Global Ethics Institute. Pamela is a Finalist for Ernst & Young's 
Entrepreneur of the Year Award. She was recently chosen as a Fast Company Expert Blogger on CSR, 
and selected as one of 50 to the White House's Consortium on Next Generation Leadership and Social 
Innovation in July 2010. 
UniversalGiving Corporate (UGC) is a customized service, helping Fortune 500 companies scale 
Corporate Social Responsibility programs worldwide. Two core services include NGO Vetting and Launch 
a City. NGO Vetting vets all nonprofits with a proprietary Quality Model, safeguarding against terrorist 
activity. Launch a City helps companies expand their CSR programs city by city, in accord with legal, 
cultural and policy ramifications in each country. UGC works with companies to strengthen their global 
brand; increase corporate employee giving and volunteer rates; increase employee retention and 
attraction; and build a stronger, more loyal client base for our corporate partners. Sample clients include 
Cisco, Symantec and MTV. 
UniversalGiving's vision is to "Create a World Where Giving and Volunteering Are a Natural Part of 
Everyday Life.• "

Business Aircraft Should Not Be Just For The Senior Execs! 
By David Wyndham 
Conklin & De Decker 
Senior executives at major forms do not need to be told the value of a business aircraft. But at 
many companies, the business aircraft seems to remain the private domain of a select few. 
The tangible benefits to having an aircraft can include, but are not limited to: 
• Time Savings 
• Flexibility and Reliability of Operations 
• Productivity 
• Ability to support customers in an effective manner 
This sort of utility can be and should be made to more than just a select few...

Winter Family Mission to Israel 
Thursday, December 22, 2011 - Sunday, January 1, 2012 
Bring your family to Israel and see what few tourists see. 
• Experience the Old City of Jerusalem and Kabbalat Shabbat at the Western Wall. 
• Connect with the diverse cultures of Israel. 
• Visit local neighborhoods and sample their cuisine. 
• Reach new heights by taking the snake path up to Masada, and new depths by taking a dip 
in the Dead Sea. 
• Explore lush, green, and freshwater nature parks or colorful sandstone mountains. 
• Survey the City of David. 
• Feel the excitement of Tel Aviv. 
• Touch the past at interactive museums, and so much more! 
Whether this is your first time in Israel or a rediscovery, there is always something new and 
wonderful to experience!
